Make sure to use this skill whenever a session of real work is ending, even if the user only says they are done. For a bare pull, push, or rebase against the remote with no session to close, use git-sync instead. ([mlarkin00/active-skills](https://github.com/mlarkin00/active-skills/tree/HEAD/close-session) · 2 copies)
- **close-session**: End-of-session wrap-up orchestrator. Runs accessibility-review, then verify-changes, fixing what each surfaces, then save-session. Use when the user wants to close out / wrap up a session cleanly in one step. ([ProstDev/skills](https://github.com/ProstDev/skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/dev/skills/close-session))
- **close-session**: Close a working session: save what it taught where the next session will read it, retire what went stale, leave nothing hanging. - **close-session**: Close out a Singularity working session: rewrite docs/state.md, add a numbered decision with its Index row when direction changed, one learnings line per failure, and a note under docs/history/. - **close-session**: Close a work session. Verifies what is really finished, commits, lands the work, cleans up and hands off to the next session. Use when the maintainer asks for it, or when every task of the session is done; never just because the context is filling up. ([starfoxcom/bindwright](https://github.com/starfoxcom/bindwright/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/close-session) · 3 copies)
- **close-sessions**: Use this skill when the Council asks to close finished agent sessions, panes, or pods, clean up idle sessions, or tidy the fleet — it closes each unit whose work has landed, including finished seats and leftover exited records, and keeps everything else.
